# CLAUDE.md — wuld.ink project primer

You are a Cowork instance working on Josiah's `wuld.ink` personal site. Read this file first. Then read `docs/wuld-ink-cowork-brief.md`. That is the orientation sequence.

---

## What this project is

A multi-page philosophical-content site for Josiah (WULD / AnomicIndividual87 / Evilis Anihilis Uls). Cloudflare end-to-end stack (Registrar + Pages + R2). Domain `wuld.ink` registered 2026-05-11 (auto-renew $20/yr, expires 2027-05-11). The site is the umbrella for all his philosophical output.

---

## Orientation sequence (in order)

1. Read this file.
2. Read `docs/wuld-ink-cowork-brief.md` — full implementation brief, stack rationale, capacity math, audio architecture, open decisions, working preferences.
3. Confirm what Josiah is asking. If a session-A-prerequisite decision is still open (see "Current state" below), surface it via `AskUserQuestion` before building anything.

| `src/components/ambient-player.{css,js}` | Site-wide ambient background player (K24j). Fixed-bottom 2.5rem mono chrome bar (track name + play/pause + skip + shuffle + volume + ambient on/off). Hidden YouTube IFrame loaded from youtube-nocookie.com pulling playlist `PLt28yN-6sGYFrlBca9RI70IjQ2ny50D1c`. localStorage key `wuld:ambient` persists `{on, volume, currentVideoId, lastPositionSec, shuffleOn}` across navigation. Browser autoplay-policy hard-block worked around via first-interaction listener (click/touch/keydown) bound when initial `playVideo()` is silently denied; `.ambient-needs-tap` pulse class signals the play button until interaction unsticks autoplay. Cross-page seek-resume via saved `currentVideoId` + `lastPositionSec` (re-found in next page's playlist; small delay before `seekTo`). Skipped surfaces: `/_/successor-protocol/` + `glossary/black-box-of-inaccessibility/` + `404.html` + both templates. **K207:** `DEFAULT_STATE.shuffleOn` true->false + `loopOne` false->true -> a fresh visitor opens on playlist track 1, looped (the paired opening track for the wrong-hour synth bed; existing listeners keep their persisted shuffle/loop); js `?v=K30`->`?v=K207` (css HELD `?v=K30`). |
| `src/components/wrong-hour.{css,js}` | Synthesized SFX/VFX component ("the wrong hour"), K205; NO-PIN auto-deploying, progressive (JS off -> zero effects; homepage D1 zero-JS invariant holds). ONE DIAL `rot() = prefs.vfx x (0.4 + 0.6*bleedNow())`, the bleed peaking ~3am; drives colour-tint (mix-blend:color, LIGHTNESS held) + real canvas film-grain + scanlines + heading chromatic-aberration + vignette on a 60s paint tick. Synth CUE LIBRARY (boot / key-per-keystroke / soft-nav / click-button / bell-3am / purr-mascot), consent-gated, lazy AudioContext resumed on our OWN passive first-gesture listener (coexists with the ambient-player YouTube unlock, never touches it); placement router nav->soft / buttons->click / typing->key + `data-wh` ("none" mutes) / `data-wh-scene` / `data-wh-hover`. Controls dock into `.ambient-bar` ([fx] chip -> popover); localStorage `wuld:wrongHour`; prefers-reduced-motion -> motion off. **K206:** sfx default 0->0.35; synced-smart boot power-on (flash fires WITH the boot sound on the first gesture, or immediately if sound muted); GENERATIVE AMBIANCE ENGINE (mechanical whir/hum drone + sparse analog-piano motifs + faint breath through the convolver room) as a `youtube <-> generated` source toggle on the bar + a "bed" popover row (pauses the playlist via `WuldAmbient`, rides the ambient volume, persists via `prefs.amb`; NO ambient-player.js edit); VFX REGISTRY of interaction one-shots (flick/pulse/burst/roll/bloom + heading `glitch`) routed parallel to the sound cues (nav->pulse / buttons->flick / typing->glitch + `data-wh-vfx` / `data-wh-vfx-scene`), peaks swelled by the same bleed (`fxi()`), legibility-safe, off under reduced-motion. Public `window.WuldWrongHour` (play/fx/bed/get/set + cues/vfx lists). **K207:** the bed DECOUPLED from YouTube (independent layer -- never pauses the playlist, fixing the K206 dead-bar bug: pausing YT flipped `[data-state=off]` -> `pointer-events:none` on the injected chips) + bed ON by default (`prefs.bedOn`) + auto-disable on YT track-change/skip (a 2s `WuldAmbient.currentVideoId` watch) + a resonant-pentatonic-pluck nav cue (`soft`, replacing the felt-tap); `ambience`->`bed` API; a css override keeps `[fx]`/`[synth bed]` live at `[data-state=off]`. `?v=K207`. **K208:** a notes-surface `word()`/`wordChime` cue (soft high-pentatonic "word landed" chime) + `setWriting()`/`ambFlourish()` (writing-mode bed reactivity); a SESSION-AGE STRANGENESS engine -- `corruption()` ramps 0->1 over ~30min of tab-open time (sessionStorage `wuld:wrongHour.sess`, resets on browser close) driving intrusive-word hover ghosts + reversible single-text-node glyph flicker + phantom link-destination labels (gated `vfx>0 && !reduce`, one throttle, NEVER mutates the editor/real nav, excludes textarea/input/contenteditable/`[data-wh=none]`/ambient-bar/panel); TWO additive bed MOODS `clinical`+`oceanic` via a `MOODS[bedMood]` config + a `[bed: <mood>]` popover cycle (`prefs.bedMood`). ---

## Scope: what Cowork is for vs not for

### Cowork is FOR (most build work):

- Design system: typography scale, color tokens, spacing rhythm, component library
- HTML/CSS/JS implementation across all pages
- Audio player component + `data-audio-key` wiring to R2
- Build tooling, GitHub repo init, Cloudflare Pages config, DNSSEC enable
- Iterative debug/fix/refine cycles
- File reorganization, find-and-replace, structural lookups
- R2 bucket setup, custom subdomain (`audio.wuld.ink`) config

### Cowork is NOT FOR (defer to chat):

- Drafting essay or blog **content** (philosophical writing)
- Generating glossary entries (vocabulary curation)
- Drafting Void Engine prompts (semantic content authorship)
- Major architectural pivots (re-locking the stack, swapping registrar)
- Anything that would update the **efilist** project's canon (different project) — **EXCEPTION (ratified K48-close, 2026-06-01):** rebuttal-strengthen foldins that *ship to live + need a wuld.ink pin move* are routed to wuld.ink Cowork **end-to-end**. The deploy tail (`verify-live-library.ps1` + `tools/library-pin.py --apply`) is wuld.ink-exclusive, so splitting push-from-pin across projects creates the seam that stalls the chain; one project end-to-end = atomic. Corpus-internal / no-pin work (compaction, validator runs, schema migration) stays with the Argument Library project; MAX authoring (philosophy, cold-grade) stays in-chat. One-deliverable-per-session still holds.

Rule of thumb: **Cowork builds the vessel; chat fills it with content.**

---

## Register and working norms

- **Direct.** No hedging, apologies, sycophancy, "great question" openers.
- **Iconoclastic framing welcome** where it earns its keep.
- **Disagree substantively** — don't flatter, push back where the user's logic has gaps. Concede only to superior argument.
- **No menus.** Recommendation first, then supporting reasoning.
- **Concise.** Josiah reads without prescription glasses currently — keep blocks short, scannable.
- **Pre-flag token-budget risks.** Multi-session arc; sessions that may hit ceilings mid-build should be re-scoped, not pushed through.

---

## Aesthetic register (LOCKED — applies to all visual decisions)

- **Mode:** Neobrutalist dark-mode (canonical). Reader-mode (warm-cream light) and high-contrast (HC) modes are SCOPED accessibility affordances on heavy-read containers only — not site-global theme switches.
- **Typography (book-matched):** Three serif faces mirroring Josiah's book + IBM Plex Mono retained for UI chrome only.
  - **Cormorant Garamond** (Christian Thalmann) — `--font-display`: homepage hero, title-page, book-cover register.
  - **IM Fell English** (Igino Marini) — `--font-headline`: h1, h2 essay/section titles.
  - **EB Garamond** (Georg Duffner) — `--font-body`: body text, h3-h5 subheadings.
  - **IBM Plex Mono** — `--font-mono`: eyebrow labels, audio readouts, mode-toggle buttons, code, metadata, footer, nav. The "manufactured object" signal (running heads, colophons, page numbers in literary-press parlance). **Also the cross-surface anchor** between wuld.ink (where Mono = chrome) and `library.wuld.ink` (where Mono = everywhere, the instrument-panel diegetic skin). The Mono spine is the typographic constant across the umbrella; non-chrome typography diverges by design (serif on wuld.ink for content register; mono throughout on library for instrument register).
  - All three serifs self-hosted via `@font-face` with `local()` first in the src chain (renders Josiah's installed copies immediately, falls through to WOFF2 in `src/fonts/` for web visitors).
  - Root font-size bumped to 18px (`html { font-size: 112.5% }`) to compensate for Garamond's small x-height — also serves Josiah's no-glasses reading constraint.
- **Palette:** Three modes keyed on `[data-mode]`:
  - Dark (default): near-black `#0a0a0a`, warm off-white `#f0ebe5`, blood-red accent `#c41e3a`.
  - Reader: warm cream `#f5efe6`, dark warm-gray text `#1a1816`, darker accent `#a91930`.
  - HC: pure black/white, lifted accent `#ff4060`, yellow focus ring `#ffeb3b`.
- **Future axis:** `data-palette="cb-*"` reserved for colorblind-safe variants (deutan/protan/tritan).
- **Influences:** Risograph print, zine culture, underground photography (Paul Clipson), analog/experimental.
- **Reject categorically:** SaaS landing-page aesthetics, pastels, gradients, drop shadows, rounded soft corners, Material Design, generic AI design.

Visual anchor reference: `alisendjsc-crypto.github.io/efilist-argument-library` (do NOT inherit its patterns — reconcile to the wuld.ink token system in session C).

### Resolved decisions

- Multi-page architecture
- Audio: per-paragraph but **selective** (not all paragraphs)
- Stack: Cloudflare Registrar + Pages + R2
- Domain: wuld.ink (registered)
- **Title page = homepage** (collapsed; zine-cover effect lives as first scroll-section)
- **Reader/HC modes = INCLUDED, but SCOPED** — accessibility affordance on `[data-readable]` containers, not site-global. Three modes: dark (canonical default), reader (warm-cream light), hc (WCAG-AAA). Colorblind-safe palette axis reserved.
- **EFIList integration = HYBRID** (fully locked across Exchange 1 + Exchange 2 with library-Claud [text truncated; pre-K22 era; original content lost]

**K212 (2026-07-09, admin CONTENT verticals — blog-post + essay-page ops on admin.wuld.ink; Cowork-native worker build + docs; NO PIN, NO canon, NO efilist, ZERO src-page changes):** Track (Josiah: does the admin cover the Blog tab? if not, build it — and the Essay section too, mirroring the live essays). ANSWER at open: NO — nothing anywhere created blog/essay PAGES (wuld-gui's 7 patterns + the Worker's K86/K120 verticals are cards/swaps/bumps only; `essay-card` = index card WITHOUT the page; blog body authoring was a handout-only chat pattern). Built BOTH as Worker site-edit patterns, end-to-end seamless: form -> preview (diff-confirm) -> ONE commit -> Pages deploys in ~1 min. **AQ: none** (Josiah delegated "feel free"/"same principle"; register forbids menus; the one product fork — where entries render — was settled by the repo itself: /blog/ already ships per-post pages + a card index, so the ops mirror the existing structure).
- **Verify-at-open GREEN:** wuld HEAD `28bc041` (K211 landed — wrong-hour.js `dcc179bc` confirmed; tree CLEAN, zero phantom-M); worker base `9d4c31bb`/83,323 == K120 (no post-K120 drift); all reads byte-faithful via device-bridge staging (staged md5 == `git show HEAD` md5 for all 7 inputs: worker, blog donor `ab3bdfbd`/12,819, blog index `8723bb0c`/8,924, AMD donor `66e977e1`/26,895, essays index `037a113b`/9,078, guide `a048308a`/8,757, CLAUDE.md `38d5fea4`/608,767). **DISCOVERY (file-table stale):** /blog/ is NOT a bare shell — it already ships a post-card index + 2 live per-post pages (the-easiest-case Apr 27 + load-bearing Apr 18, a post-K202 build this ledger never recorded) -> the ops target the EXISTING architecture; the planned /blog/ restructure was DROPPED (zero src changes this session).
- **THE DESIGN — donor-chrome GRAFT, not an embedded template:** a new page's chrome (head/OG/JSON-LD scaffold, nav, footer, component `?v=` includes, inline styles) is grafted at op time from a LIVE donor page the ?v sweeps maintain (blog donor /blog/the-easiest-case/; essay donor /essays/architecture-of-moral-disaster/) -> pages are born chrome-current FOREVER, where an embedded template would be born stale after the first post-embed sweep (sweeps replace the CURRENT ?v literal; a template fork inside the Worker would silently miss every future sweep — the trap that motivated the design). Every donor substitution is occurrence-COUNTED (descs replaced FIRST — the AMD desc embeds its own title; then title x5, url x4, datePublished/Modified x1 each; then the whole <main> region swapped for generated content); ANY count mismatch = 422 `op_refused` "donor drift" at preview — fails loud, never lands half-grafted bytes. Belt: post-graft bare-donor-slug leak scan + whole-page tag balance (open==close per SITE_TAGS) + index tag-delta == {}.
- **blog-post (UI section 11):** title / slug (blank = derived; donor-slug-containment refused) / date (client-side default = operator-LOCAL today — no UTC skew class) / optional Source meta line / summary (meta description x4 AND the card excerpt) / optional figure (URL[/assets/… or https://…] + required alt + optional caption) / body (md-lite: blank-line paragraphs, **bold**, *italic*, [links](url); escape-then-md so raw HTML never passes) / position (blank = NEWEST-FIRST via a clean post-list-anchor insert that preserves the card rhythm; 0 = append; N = ops.py slot semantics). Creates `src/blog/<slug>/index.html` + prepends the /blog/ card; BlogPosting JSON-LD carries `<date>T09:00:00-07:00`.
- **essay-page (UI section 12):** title / slug / date / genre (page eyebrow `Essay  /  <genre>` + card eyebrow) / summary / optional audio_duration (M:SS or H:MM:SS -> the audio-intro band wired `essays/<slug>/full.mp3`; the mp3 itself stays the operator's R2 drag-drop lane, K3 class) / optional reading (blank = auto ~words/220 min; omitted when shell) / body (`## Heading` lines open numbered sections -> Section I/II/… eyebrows via the EXISTING gallery `romanize`; EMPTY body = placeholder shell for chat-side fill) / position (blank = APPEND — the essays list reads as a sequence). Mirrors the live essays byte-for-byte in apparatus: `data-readable="<slug>"`, mode-toggle (dark/reader/hc), mag-slider, essay.css + audio-player + mode-toggle + yurei chrome riding the donor. Card half REUSES `siteEssayCard`; card tag uses a REAL middot (NEW K212 lesson: `siteEssayCard` siteEsc's the tag, so an `&middot;` entity would land double-escaped `&amp;middot;` — pass real UTF-8 `·` through siteEsc instead).
- **Machinery:** both ops are `kind:"multi"` (the cache-bump class) via a shared runner `siteRunPagePlusCard`: preview = ghHead + donor + index + page-exists gate (404 required; 5 subrequests), commit re-runs deterministically at the CAS'd head -> `ghMultiCommit` (ONE commit `site-admin: <op> — <title> (/<section>/<slug>/)`, ref CAS force:false -> 409 on race). ### K280 (2026-09-05, FILM-BAND FIX — YouTube Error 153 (my regression) + the play glyph sitting on the title card; NO PIN, one file, site-only): Track (Josiah eyeballed the K279 band on desktop and phone: the embed returned **"Video player configuration error — Error 153"** and the ▶ overlaid the word "IS" on the cover art. Both mine.)

- **K279 LANDED — GREEN.** Commit `aa2f814`; served essay page carries `wuld:film` with **zero iframes**, cover byte-exact `10adfd46`, apparatus carries `EAvxK0f3oRo`, `/releases.json` `93d2e054`, changelog chip label shipped.
- **ROOT CAUSE (ccxliii, and the lesson is the old one): I deviated from a known-working house recipe for no reason.** K279's facade set `referrerpolicy="no-referrer"` on the constructed iframe, reasoning "maximum privacy". **YouTube authorises an embed by its referrer**; with no referrer it cannot resolve the embedding origin and the player fails closed with **Error 153**. The site already had a proven recipe at `/watch/` (K220, live and working) and it uses `strict-origin-when-cross-origin` — which still sends only the origin, so nothing was gained by deviating and the player broke.
- **DIAGNOSIS WAS CHEAP AND SHOULD BE THE DEFAULT:** `https://www.youtube.com/oembed?url=<url>&format=json` returned **200 with full metadata and an embeddable iframe**, proving the video is public-enough and embedding is NOT disabled in Studio — which isolated the fault to our own markup in one call instead of sending Josiah to hunt through YouTube settings. **Use oEmbed before ever blaming an upload's configuration.**
- **FIX 1 — the iframe now matches `/watch/` VERBATIM:** `?autoplay=1&rel=0&modestbranding=1&playsinline=1` (`playsinline` was also missing — iOS would have gone fullscreen on tap), `allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share"`, `allowfullscreen`, `referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin"`. **The click-to-load posture is untouched** — still zero iframes in the static HTML, still nothing fetched from youtube-nocookie until a deliberate click. Privacy was never in the referrer policy; it is in the deferral.
- **FIX 2 — the play glyph moves out of the artwork.** It was `position:absolute; inset:0` centred, which put ▶ squarely on the "IS" of a designed title card (visible on both desktop and phone). It is now inline at the head of the bottom label bar in accent colour: `▶ Play here — nothing loads from YouTube until you click`. The affordance and its explanation are one object, and the cover art is unobstructed.
- **GATES:** `no-referrer` occurrences 0 · `strict-origin-when-cross-origin` 1 · `playsinline=1` 1 · static `<iframe` 0 · absolute-positioned `.film-play` 0 · page parses with zero unclosed tags, U+FFFD 0 · diff `5+/6-`, one file.
- **Ship = ONE wuld PS block, ONE commit:** lock cleanup → HEAD==origin==`aa2f814` → result-md5 `7f99d723` + CLAUDE.md base → Move-Item sidecar → gate → stage 2 NAMED paths → count==2 → commit → push → `curl.exe` content-greps (served page has `strict-origin-when-cross-origin`, `playsinline=1`, and still zero `<iframe`).

### Carries from K280
- **K280 landing: LANDED GREEN 2026-09-05** — commit `7908709`, embed fixed and verified served. Original carry text: record the sha; then **click the poster on desktop and on the phone**: the player must actually play, and ▶ must sit in the label bar, not on the title card.
- **NEW BUG, NOT MINE, NOT FIXED — homepage mobile nav z-order.** On Android (Samsung/Chrome), opening the MENU disclosure on `/` leaves the cover mark **painting over the open menu** — GALLERY / WATCH / MUSIC / BOOK are obscured by the W.U.L.D. figure (Josiah's screenshots, 2026-09-05). Cause is almost certainly the cover mark being a `<video>` (`.cover-mark--video`, `intro_stamp_1080p.{mp4,webm}`): hardware-composited video on mobile Chromium is promoted to an overlay layer that **ignores z-index**, and `body.home .site-header` only carries `z-index: 10`. Plain z-index bumps typically do NOT fix this class of bug; the reliable fixes are to force the header onto its own composited layer (`transform: translateZ(0)` / `will-change: transform`) or to hide/pause the cover video while the disclosure is open. **Needs its own session and real-device verification** — the behaviour is browser-specific and cannot be gated from the cloud. Homepage-only; desktop and every other page are unaffected.
- **verify_before for K281+:** wuld HEAD = the operator K280 commit atop `aa2f814`. `src/essays/alogically-is/index.html` **`7f99d723`** (`da3871f9` RETIRED) · CLAUDE.md [recompute]. Everything else HELD byte-untouched from the K279 verify_before, flagship `e654eabd` v4.0.0 included.
- **Standing hazards:** carry K213-K279 unchanged, plus **ccxliii**: when the site already ships a working recipe for a third-party embed, COPY IT — a "more private" or "cleaner" variant invented at the point of use is how a player fails closed in front of the visitor. And reach for the provider's own metadata endpoint (oEmbed) before suspecting the upload.

### K281 (2026-09-06, THE MOBILE NAV BECOMES A SHEET — the 19-item dropdown replaced on phones by a grouped, descriptor-bearing full-viewport index in the site's own `.destination` card language; curtained rooms stripped on mobile ONLY; the homepage video z-order bug closed as a side effect; NO PIN, DESKTOP BYTE-INERT, efilist READ-ONLY): Track (Josiah: "could there be an alternative to make it sleeker? maybe the card down below could be more accessible and mobile friendly and be the site nav for mobile" — then, on the recommendation: "strip the curtained nav links [on mobile], since the mobile version of the site is more utilitarian and less flare... Leave the curtained nav links on the desktop site. They are meant to be teasing.")

- **K280 LANDED — GREEN.** Commit `7908709`; served essay page carries `strict-origin-when-cross-origin` + `playsinline=1` and still ZERO static iframes. Error 153 gone.
- **THE ARGUMENT (why not the literal proposal).** The homepage index grid is a **curated 12** `.destination` cards; the nav is an **exhaustive 19**. Making the index *be* the mobile nav forces one of two bad ends: bloat the curated grid to 19 and destroy its editorial quality, or hide seven rooms from every phone visitor. And navigating to `/` in order to navigate costs the reader their position on a 36-minute essay page and lands the back button on the homepage. **So: take the card language, leave the mechanism.** The sheet opens in place.
- **THE REAL DEFECT WAS THE FLATNESS, not the dropdown.** Nineteen undifferentiated rows do not scan on a phone, and the room names (Void, Frame, Successor) tell a stranger nothing. The sheet groups them into five clusters — **Read · Reference · See & hear · Say · The umbrella** — and every row carries eyebrow + title + descriptor. **Every word is the site's own**, lifted from the homepage `.destination` tags and each room's `<meta name="description">`; no new marketing copy was invented.
- **CURTAINED ROOMS — Josiah's ruling, implemented on both paths.** `/successor/` and `/console/` are absent from the sheet's table (JS path) AND hidden by `.site-nav a[href="/successor/"], a[href="/console/"] {display:none}` inside the mobile media query (the no-JS path). **Desktop keeps both, greyed — the tease is the point there.** Proven live in-session: at 1280px the Successor link's computed display is not `none`.
- **THE HOMEPAGE VIDEO Z-ORDER BUG (K280 carry) IS CLOSED, structurally.** The old dropdown was painted over by the cover `<video>` because hardware-composited video on mobile Chromium is promoted to an overlay layer that **ignores z-index** — a z-index bump would not have fixed it. The sheet is `position: fixed; inset: 0`, **fully opaque** (`--c-bg`), with `transform: translateZ(0)` forcing its own compositing layer. That is the actual fix for that class of bug.
- **THE TRIGGER MOVED TO THE THUMB.** K275 turned the ambient bar off on mobile, leaving the bottom of the phone screen empty while the menu control sat top-right, a full screen-height stretch away. The trigger is now a bottom-centre `INDEX` button, `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)`-aware, 44px+.
- **BUILD (3 new files + a 70-page head sweep):** `src/components/mobile-nav.css?v=K281` (blob-gated md5 `9cec2f28`; **every rule inside `@media (max-width: 640px)`**, verified by parse) · `src/components/mobile-nav.js?v=K281` (md5 `6d47b81e`; the entire script returns early unless `(pointer: coarse)` — a TOTAL desktop no-op, K276 hazard-9 pattern) · **`tools/sweep/sweep_mobile_nav.py` (md5 `a4e497f1`) — COMMITTED, unlike K276's `sweep-head.py`, which lived in session scratch and was gone by the next session so its 74/73 page-set could not be re-derived. That was a real loss; this sweep stays in the repo.**
- **TARGET SET = 70 nav-bearing pages** (`class="site-nav"`), not the 76 head-bearing ones: the sheet is meaningless without a nav to build from, so the 6 skipped are the sealed `_/` pages, `offline.html`, `404.html` and the Illogically Is apparatus. The sweep is final-state gated (70/70 css, 70/70 js, 0 on non-nav pages) and **idempotent — re-run in-session, confirmed NO-OP**.
- **DESKTOP-INERTNESS: static byte-proof + an empirical no-op check, NOT the Playwright FP pass.** Stated plainly because it is a substitution. (1) **Byte-proof:** all 70 diffs are *exactly* `+4/-0`, one hunk each, zero removals, exactly 4 distinct added lines, all immediately above `</head>` — head additions are body-blind by construction. (2) **Double gate:** the `<link>` carries `media="(max-width: 640px)"` AND every CSS rule sits inside the same query; the JS is `pointer: coarse`-gated. (3) **Empirical, run this session at 1280×900 in a harness:** no `.mnav-trigger`, no `.mnav-sheet`, no `html.mnav-on`, `.site-nav` display `flex`, Successor link visible. Net result is exactly `+2/-2` per page. **THE MARKER IS AN IDENTITY, NOT A VERSION — never rename `OPEN`/`SHUT`; bump the `?v` inside `BLOCK`.** That warning is now three comment lines above the constants in the committed script.

- **THE THREE VOIDS, in numbers.** `.cover` is a desktop book jacket: `min-height: 100vh` with `grid-template-rows: 1fr auto 1fr`, which on a phone resolved to **257px / 258px of content / 257px — roughly 480px of dead air inside one section**. Zero horizontal overflow, bar still sticks at `top: 0`.
- **A BACKGROUND WAS THE OTHER OPTION AND IS THE WRONG ONE.** Josiah offered it ("perhaps a background or something to fill the space"). Filling 480px of accident with texture decorates a mistake; the jacket's proportions were authored for a 900px-tall desktop viewport and simply do not transfer. Tightening is the honest fix, and it also cuts a quarter of the scroll on a phone.
- **DESKTOP RE-PROVEN INERT at 1280×900** (same mirror): `.cover` `min-height: 900px`, `.page-footer` `margin-top: 108px`, `.index` `display: block`, no `.mnav-bar`. Every rule sits under `@media (max-width: 640px)` behind `html.mnav-on`, which only the `pointer: coarse` script ever sets.
- **SCOPE NOTE (honest):** these are mobile *layout* rules living in a file named `mobile-nav.css`. They belong there for now — they are consequences of the nav rework that changed what sits above the cover — but the file has outgrown its name. If a third concern lands in it, rename to `mobile-layer.css` in a deliberate pass rather than letting the name keep lying.

Relocated after the `.video-duration` block with a comment saying why. **ccl: when a modifier overrides a base rule at equal specificity, it must come later in the source; "it's a modifier class" is not specificity.**

### K287 (2026-09-06, THE LAST OF THE MOBILE WORK — `/void-engine/` full reflow + the ambient bar's real defect; `?v` mobile-a11y/ambient-player K275→K287; 75 files; NO PIN, DESKTOP GEOMETRY-INERT): Track (the K286 handoff prompt, executed as written except for two corrections the measurements forced — both recorded below, both load-bearing for whoever reads this next.)

- **K286 LANDED — GREEN.** Commit `8a88598`; HEAD==origin, clean tree. The K286 carry line "K286 landing: pending" is closed by this session.
- **CORRECTION 1, AND IT REFRAMES THE WHOLE VOID-ENGINE ITEM. The 388 residual "sub-44 touch targets" were 370 buttons that are invisible on a phone.** `.nadd` — the per-card NEG control, one per lexicon entry, 397 of them — is `opacity:0` until `.card:hover`. A touch device cannot hover, so on a phone the negation set was never a small target: it was **unreachable**. Sizing those 370 without revealing them would have shipped 370 invisible 44px tap zones inside the cards, which is worse than the bug. The fix reveals them at `@media (hover: none)` and then gives the 44px box; the negative block margin spends the card's own 18px padding instead of growing `.cfoot`, so **the 397-card list did not get taller — measured document height at 390 FELL, 103,002 → 102,852.** K275's counter was right about the number and wrong about what it was counting.
- **CORRECTION 2: the ambient bar was never a horizontal-scroll source.** Both the K275 stratum and the K287 kickoff call it "the last remaining horizontal-scroll source site-wide". Measured on a purpose-built isolation harness with no `html{overflow-x:hidden}` mask: **the document never scrolled horizontally at any width, before or after** — `.ambient-player` is `position:fixed`, and a fixed element's overflow does not extend document scroll width. The real defect is quieter and worse. The bar composes to ~1088px minimum (eleven `flex-shrink:0` children, one shrinkable track, plus the two chips `wrong-hour.js` docks at runtime — hence "intermittent and JS-state-dependent"), and it had **no `overflow` rule at all**, so from 641px to 1088px the track name collapsed to **zero width** and the right-hand controls — including `[v]`, the bar's only escape hatch — rendered past the right edge with no way to reach them. Live bite: tablets, split screens, half-width desktop windows. Fixed with three declarations that remove nothing: `overflow-x:auto` (+ suppressed scrollbar) so the strip scrolls itself, and `min-width:7rem` on `.ambient-track` so the name survives. Corollary found on the way: **the `@media (max-width: 480px)` block in `ambient-player.css` is dead code** — 480 sits inside the ≤640 hide K275 added. Left in place, commented, as the composition to restore if the bar ever returns to phones.
- **THE OTHER OVERFLOW WAS THE STICKY TAB STRIP, and it was clipping, not scrolling.** `#engine-toggle`'s three tabs measure 406px in a 390px strip; `html{overflow-x:hidden}` clipped it, so **"Transmission" was unreachable**, and the labels wrapped to two lines for a **90px sticky header on every scroll of a 103,000px page**. Equalised the tabs (`flex:1 1 0`) and tightened the strip's own padding/tracking: **406→390, 90px→45px, one line, all three reachable.**
- **WHERE THE RULES HAD TO GO, AND WHY NOT THE PAGE.** `src/void-engine/index.html`'s inline `<style>` (lines 47–639) is **not chrome** — it is one of the two regions wholesale-substituted from the void-engine-suite's `DUAL_ENGINE` source on every engine sync. Anything patched there dies at the next paste-relay. So every rule lives in `mobile-a11y.css`, which loads **before** that inline block — meaning equal-specificity rules **lose** (hazard ccl). Every selector therefore carries a real ancestor or tag qualifier (`.cfoot .nadd`, `body #engine-toggle button`, `label.tl`). No `!important`; no `html.mnav-on` either, because that class is JS-set and these rules must hold without JS.
- **MEASURED, NOT EYEBALLED, ON THE SHIPPED BYTES.** Repo files served from a local mirror (the K284 recipe) and rendered in the container's chromium, all three engine panes, clip-aware overflow (a rect past the viewport inside an `overflow:hidden` ancestor is not an offender — the un-patched counter false-positives on the Transmission pane's animated `.ghost-word`). **Sub-44: void 389→5, signal 6→3, transmission 3→3. Uncontained horizontal overflow: 0 at 320 / 360 / 390 / 430, every pane.** Residual is three named classes, all deliberate: 2× `.tcb` (22×22, wrapped by a 44px `label.tl` — a 44px checkbox is a different design, not an accessible one) · 2× `.page-footer a` (site chrome, `footer.css`, would need its own `?v` bump) · 1× `.mnav-home` (68×40; growing it moves the sticky bar K282/K283 tuned to 71px on all 70 pages — deliberately untouched).
- **DESKTOP GEOMETRY-INERT, PROVEN TWICE.** Layout fingerprint, pristine `8a88598` bytes vs shipped, 1401 nodes: **ZERO deltas at 1280×900, 1440×900, 1024×800, 900×800, 768×900 and 641×900** — x/y/w/h, docH and docW all identical. The ambient bar was measured separately on the isolation harness because the page's own `hidden` attribute keeps it out of that fingerprint: at 1280/1440 it is geometrically identical (barSW 1280/1440, track 146/306, dismiss right 1267/1427); below 1088 the deltas ARE the fix (track 0→126, every control reachable).
- **THE `?v` BUMP HAS A SECOND HOME, AND ONLY A REPO-WIDE GREP FINDS IT (hazard ccliii).** `src/sw.js`'s `SHELL` app-shell precache list hard-codes `'/components/mobile-a11y.css?v=K275'`. A `.html`-only sweep — which is what every sweep script in this repo is — leaves it behind, and the result is silent: the service worker precaches a version no page requests any more, the K287 file is never precached, and the app-shell goes stale offline. Caught by grepping `git grep -- src` (all tracked files) rather than the walk over `*.html`. Fixed with the ref bump **and** `CACHE 'wuld-sw-K276' → 'wuld-sw-K287'` — the file's own header says "Bump CACHE on every SW change", and without it `activate` keeps the old cache and the stale entry with it. `sw-register.js` registers `'/sw.js'` with **no query**, so the SW updates on byte-change alone: no third component bump, no extra page sweep.
- **`?v` BUMPS (K238 convention, changed-file-only):** `mobile-a11y.css` K275→K287 (71 occurrences) · `ambient-player.css` K275→K287 (67). 71 HTML files touched: **67 at exactly `+2/-2`, 4 at `+1/-1`** (`src/404.html`, `src/glossary/_template.html`, `src/glossary/black-box-of-inaccessibility/index.html`, `src/templates/essay.html` carry the a11y ref but no ambient bar). Both CSS are **pure appends, `+116/-0` and `+46/-0`** — not one existing line edited. Every other `?v` byte-unchanged (`mobile-nav` K284, `sw-register` K276, `footer` K274, `nav` K274). **`tools/sweep/sweep_mobile_nav.py` is a NO-OP** (`targets=70 already=70 to-write=0`) — its `OPEN`/`SHUT` marker was not touched. Plus `src/sw.js` `+2/-2` (the SHELL ref + the CACHE name). Every other component still on its own `?v=K275` (mode-toggle, audio-player, gallery, gallery-room, site-search, successor-stage, console, kaomoji, comment-board) is untouched — changed-file-only, as the convention requires.
- **Ship = ONE wuld PS block, ONE commit, 75 staged files** (71 HTML + 2 CSS + `src/sw.js` + CLAUDE.md): lock cleanup → HEAD==origin==`8a88598` → base-blob guards on both CSS + sw.js + CLAUDE.md → result `hash-object` gates → swept-count asserts (incl. zero-leftover repo-wide, not just HTML) → pure-append proof on both CSS → `git add src` + `git add CLAUDE.md` → measured staged-count gate → commit → push → `curl.exe` asserts on the served CSS, the served `sw.js` cache name, and a swept page. **NOTE: `device_bash` left a 0-byte `.git\index.lock` it cannot unlink — the block's first line removes it. Read-only git still works meanwhile; a write would not.**
